## Severity

- `critical`: unsafe fact, fabricated/misattributed PYQ or key, major syllabus omission, or material
  contradiction that makes the package unreliable.
- `high`: missing Core mechanism/evidence, weak model-answer capability, Advanced/Core leakage, or
  major flowchart omission.
- `medium`: incomplete practice coverage, weak explanation, missing qualification, or local visual
  compression that reduces learning or answer quality.
- `low`: non-blocking wording, navigation, repetition or presentation improvement.

Close an issue only after its affected artifacts have been regenerated where necessary and the
topic has passed revalidation.
